Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the waves framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 457. The resulting operator achieves bound O(781/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the waves space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 457 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 781. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The waves variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 999 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 340 and intersection form of rank 4.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
